never coerce to nearest valid state.\r\n- **Retry**：only explicitly retryable transient events; retry budget=3 unless stricter domain budget; semantic/authority/security failures are non-retryable.\r\n- **Timeout**：active nonterminal state must carry deadline; timeout routes to BLOCKED/EXPIRED/KILLED/REJECTED as appropriate.\r\n- **Requalification**：expiry, revocation, source/version drift or rollback removes prior qualification and requires the corresponding admission path again.\r\n\r\n|event|from|to|guard|effect / failure branch|\r\n|---|---|---|---|---|\r\n|ADMIT|DRAFT|ADMITTED|entry/dependency/authority complete|persist transition/evidence; unmet guard → INVALID_TRANSITION and no canonical mutation|\r\n|START|READY|RUNNING|lease+permission+checkpoint valid|persist transition/evidence; unmet guard → INVALID_TRANSITION and no canonical mutation|\r\n|VERIFY|RUNNING|VERIFYING|tasks ended and evidence complete|persist transition/evidence; unmet guard → INVALID_TRANSITION and no canonical mutation|\r\n|PASS|VERIFYING|PASS|independent oracle passes|persist transition/evidence; unmet guard → INVALID_TRANSITION and no canonical mutation|\r\n|REPAIR|VERIFYING|REPAIR|functional/schema finding is repairable|persist transition/evidence; unmet guard → INVALID_TRANSITION and no canonical mutation|\r\n|BLOCK|RUNNING|BLOCKED|security/authority/non-retryable failure|persist transition/evidence; unmet guard → INVALID_TRANSITION and no canonical mutation|\r\n|CANCEL|RUNNING|CANCELLED|authorized cancel; cleanup receipt required|persist transition/evidence; unmet guard → INVALID_TRANSITION and no canonical mutation|\r\n|CLOSE|PASS|CLOSED|receiver ACK + scoped gate complete|persist transition/evidence; unmet guard → INVALID_TRANSITION and no canonical mutation|\r\n\r\n`status: SM-01_FINAL_R2_DEFINED / NOT_EXECUTED`\r\n\r\n#### SM-02｜TaskLease\r\n\r\n- **States**：CREATED → OFFERED → ACCEPTED → ACTIVE → RENEWED → EXPIRED → REVOKED → RETURNED → CLOSED\r\n- **Invalid transition**：reject event, preserve canonical state, emit finding/evidence; generation, permission profile]\r\n  setup: [workspace, fixtures, mock/isolated dependencies]\r\n  ordered_steps: []\r\n  mutation_or_negative: []\r\n  expected_events: []\r\n  deterministic_oracle: {}\r\n  judge_policy: deterministic_first / calibrated_llm_only_when_needed\r\n  evidence_paths: []\r\n  cleanup: []\r\n  rollback: []\r\n  independent_verifier: required\r\n  run_status: PLANNED_NOT_RUN\r\n```\r\n\r\nRunner contract：`load frozen fixture → verify precondition hashes → run case-specific ordered_steps (not generic RBWI stub-marker) → inject declared negative/mutation → capture state/effect/evidence transitions → evaluate deterministic oracle → cleanup/rollback → independent verdict`。若 case 沒有具體 `ordered_steps` 或 domain oracle，只能 `DESIGNED_INCOMPLETE`，不得以 registry row existence 視為 test-ready。\r\n\r\n# Block 4 — Governance\r\n\r\n\r\n## 4.1｜SoD and Human Approval\r\n\r\n|decision/effect|maker|checker/security|independent verifier|human approver|release same worker|\r\n|Security acceptance|control maker|F16 checker|F16-06/F15-06 separate identity|F01-02 risk acceptance|F17-06|security maker self-pass|\r\n|Independent acceptance|domain maker Spine、Human Authority、SoD、security、acceptance 或 rollback 要求，則不是 fallback，而是 CR。Adoption state 只允許 `PREFERRED_DEFAULT | APPROVED_ALTERNATIVE | DONOR_PATTERN_ONLY | DEFERRED_BY_EVIDENCE | REJECTED`；runtime axis 另行記錄 `NOT_INSPECTED | QUALIFIED_SCOPE | REJECTED_RUNTIME`。\r\n\r\n## 5.4｜Route-out／Handoff Contract\r\n\r\n```yaml\r\nroute_out_packet:\r\n  route_out_decision: artifact_version_environment_commands_inputs_outputs_logs_tests_security_checksum_timestamp_deviations_tt_rollback\r\n  rollback_contract: pre_state_target_actions_verify_receiver\r\n  receiver_ack: ACK_OR_NACK_WITH_REASON_AND_EVIDENCE\r\n```\r\n\r\n## 5.5｜TEMP_CLOSED and Reopen Conditions\r\n\r\n本全冊藍圖在作者側可達 `STRUCTURE_PASS + BODY_SUBSTANTIVE_AUTHOR_SIDE_PATCH_COMPLETE + LOCAL_PACKAGE_PASS`；由 BT-001～BT-014 所覆蓋的 runtime、provider、Windows、HLPE round-trip、Hermes pilot、MCP、Skills、security、92 cases、DR、platform drift、Human ReleaseDecision 均保持 `TEMP_CLOSED/NOT_EXECUTED/NOT_RUN/NOT_CLAIMED`。Reopen 僅在具名 owner 提交對應 close evidence，經 checker/independent verifier/Human authority 所需 gate 後發生。\r\n\r\n## 5.6｜r2 Author-side Upgrade Completion Receipt\r\n\r\n|item|result|\r\n|---|---|\r\n|Selected ..\\PIPD\\src\\pipd_ls_sp\\abi.py\r\n\"\"\"The Need / Occupant ABI, and the Consumer that goes through it.\r\n\r\nWP-FHS-13 was blocked on `ABI_CONSUMER_MISSING`. `tools/hotswap.py` implements the swap itself (preimage → bind →\r\nstage → swap → control → POS → NEG → rollback, 5/5 tests), but a swap needs three things it did not have:\r\n\r\n    Need      what a consumer requires of a capability: a name and a contract version\r\n    Occupant  what a provider offers: the implementation that satisfies a Need\r\n    Consumer  the code that resolves a Need to an Occupant at call time\r\n\r\nWithout the third, a hot swap can only be checked by running a separate process and reading its output -- which\r\nproves that *some* entry point changed, not that the consumer the system actually uses now reaches the new occupant.\r\nThat distinction is the whole point of the work package, so this module makes the consumer the thing under test.\r\n\r\nDesign rules, each one a defect this file exists to prevent:\r\n\r\n  * A version mismatch is a refusal, never a best-effort call. An occupant that answers a different contract is\r\n    indistinguishable from a working one until it corrupts something.\r\n  * Resolution happens per call, not at construction. A consumer that binds once cannot observe a swap -- it is a\r\n    cached answer pretending to be a live lookup.\r\n  * An unsatisfied need raises a named error. Returning None turns a wiring fault into a TypeError three frames away.\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport importlib.util\r\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\r\n\r\nABI_VERSION = 1\r\n\r\n\r\nclass AbiError(Exception):\r\n    \"\"\"Base for ABI faults; each subclass carries a stable code.\"\"\"\r\n\r\n    code = \"ERR_ABI\"\r\n\r\n\r\nclass AbiUnsatisfied(AbiError):\r\n    code = \"ERR_ABI_UNSATISFIED\"\r\n\r\n\r\nclass AbiVersionMismatch(AbiError):\r\n    code = \"ERR_ABI_VERSION_MISMATCH\"\r\n\r\n\r\nclass AbiDuplicateOccupant(AbiError):\r\n    code = \"ERR_ABI_DUPLICATE_OCCUPANT\"\r\n\r\n\r\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\r\nclass Need:\r\n    \"\"\"What a consumer requires. `version` is part of the requirement, not metadata.\"\"\"\r\n\r\n    name: str\r\n    version: int = ABI_VERSION\r\n    methods: tuple[str, ...] = ()\r\n\r\n    def satisfied_by(self, occupant: \"Occupant\") -> bool:\r\n        if occupant.need.name != self.name:\r\n            return False\r\n        if occupant.need.version != self.version:\r\n            return False\r\n        return all(hasattr(occupant.impl, m) for m in self.methods)\r\n\r\n\r\n@dataclass\r\nclass Occupant:\r\n    \"\"\"A provider bound to a Need. `impl` is any object exposing the needed methods.\"\"\"\r\n\r\n    need: Need\r\n    impl: Any\r\n    origin: str = \"\"\r\n\r\n    def call(self, method: str, *args, **kwargs):\r\n        if not hasattr(self.impl, method):\r\n            raise AbiUnsatisfied(f\"{self.origin or type(self.impl).__name__} has no method {method!r}\")\r\n        return getattr(self.impl, method)(*args, **kwargs)\r\n\r\n\r\n@dataclass\r\nclass Registry:\r\n    \"\"\"Need-name → Occupant. Deliberately not a global: two registries in one process must not see each other.\"\"\"\r\n\r\n    occupants: dict[str, Occupant] = field(default_factory=dict)\r\n\r\n    def bind(self, occupant: Occupant, *, replace: bool = False) -> None:\r\n        name = occupant.need.name\r\n        if name in self.occupants and not replace:\r\n            raise AbiDuplicateOccupant(f\"{name!r} is already bound by {self.occupants[name].origin!r}\")\r\n        self.occupants[name] = occupant\r\n\r\n    def resolve(self, need: Need) -> Occupant:\r\n        occ = self.occupants.get(need.name)\r\n        if occ is None:\r\n            raise AbiUnsatisfied(f\"no occupant for need {need.name!r}\")\r\n        if not need.satisfied_by(occ):\r\n            raise AbiVersionMismatch(\r\n                f\"need {need.name!r} v{need.version} is not satisfied by {occ.origin or 'occupant'} \"\r\n                f\"v{occ.need.version}\" + (f\" missing {[m for m in need.methods if not hasattr(occ.impl, m)]}\"\r\n                                          if need.methods else \"\"))\r\n        return occ\r\n\r\n\r\ndef occupant_from_file(need: Need, path: Path, *, factory: str = \"build\") -> Occupant:\r\n    \"\"\"Load an occupant from a Python file. This is the shape a hot swap produces: the file changes, the Need does not.\"\"\"\r\n    path = Path(path)\r\n    if not path.exists():\r\n        raise AbiUnsatisfied(f\"occupant file {path} does not exist\")\r\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(f\"_occupant_{need.name}_{abs(hash(str(path)))}\", path)\r\n    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:\r\n        raise AbiUnsatisfied(f\"cannot load occupant from {path}\")\r\n    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\r\n    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)\r\n    if not hasattr(mod, factory):\r\n        raise AbiUnsatisfied(f\"{path} has no factory {factory!r}\")\r\n    return Occupant(need=need, impl=getattr(mod, factory)(), origin=str(path))\r\n\r\n\r\nclass Consumer:\r\n    \"\"\"The code the system actually calls.\r\n\r\n    `registry` is consulted on every call, so replacing the occupant is visible to a consumer that was constructed\r\n    before the swap. A consumer that caches the occupant cannot tell you whether a swap took effect, and that is\r\n    exactly the question a hot swap has to answer.\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n\r\n    def __init__(self, need: Need, registry: Registry):\r\n        self.need = need\r\n        self.registry = registry\r\n\r\n    def __call__(self, method: str, *args, **kwargs):\r\n        return self.registry.resolve(self.need).call(method, *args, **kwargs)\r\n\r\n    def serves(self, method: str, *args, **kwargs) -> dict:\r\n        \"\"\"POS/NEG probe: does the consumer, as wired, reach a working occupant?\"\"\"\r\n        try:\r\n            value = self(method, *args, **kwargs)\r\n            return {\"ok\": True, \"value\": value, \"origin\": self.registry.resolve(self.need).origin}\r\n        except AbiError as exc:\r\n            return {\"ok\": False, \"code\": exc.code, \"why\": str(exc)}\r\n\r\n\r\ndef make_consumer(need: Need, occupant_path: Path, *, factory: str = \"build\") -> Consumer:\r\n    \"\"\"Convenience wiring: one need, one occupant file, one consumer.\"\"\"\r\n    reg = Registry()\r\n    reg.bind(occupant_from_file(need, occupant_path, factory=factory))\r\n    return Consumer(need, reg)\r\nFILE ..\\PIPD\\tests\\test_abi.py\r\n\"\"\"The consumer must observe the swap; a version mismatch must refuse rather than call.\r\n\r\nThe claim WP-FHS-13 needs is narrow and checkable: after the occupant behind a Need is replaced, the *same consumer\r\nobject* answers with the new implementation and no longer with the old one. Twenty files mention the word and every one of them is a QA repair tool or an officer verify script.\r\nWP-FHS-13 therefore fails on `CAPABILITY_PARITY_NOT_PROVEN`, which is the spec's own first-fail for \"熱替代 Issuer\r\n或以選型當 invoke\" — proving a swap happened is not the same as proving the new occupant serves and the old one\r\nstopped serving.\r\n\r\nThis tool does the swap and proves the parity, in that order, on a NAMED target root:\r\n\r\n    preimage    snapshot every file under --root with its digest\r\n    bind        the current occupant must be the one we expect (else ERR_OCCUPANT_MISMATCH, no effect)\r\n    stage       copy the candidate beside the target and verify its digest before anything moves\r\n    swap        old -> .prev, staged -> target (os.replace, same volume)\r\n    POS         invoke the NEW occupant; its identity must equal --expect-new\r\n    NEG         invoke the OLD occupant path; it must no longer serve the old identity\r\n    rollback    if POS or NEG fails, restore .prev and verify the preimage byte-for-byte\r\n\r\nExit 0 only when POS and NEG both hold. The\r\n    work package asks whether the consumer the system actually calls reaches the new occupant. That is a different\r\n    question and it has a different failure mode: a consumer that binds once keeps serving the old implementation\r\n    after a perfect file swap. This tool removes the field as the\r\nsource of truth: a grant is now valid because an Ed25519 signature over its own payload verifies against a key id,\r\nthe nonce has never been spent, and the TTL has not expired. `assurance_level` is derived from *which key signed it*,\r\nnever typed in.\r\n\r\nKey handling, stated plainly:\r\n\r\n* The private key lives at `config/issuer/issuer.key`, 32 raw seed bytes, and is protected by **DPAPI** when the\r\n  platform allows it (Windows encrypts it to the owner's own logon — no passphrase to type, nothing in the chat, and\r\n  the file is useless if copied to another account). Where DPAPI is unavailable it stays a plain seed file and the\r\n  receipt says so.\r\n* `key_id = sha256(public_key)[:16]`. Rotation is a new file, never an edited one.\r\n* Every issued grant consumes a nonce written to `config/issuer/nonce-ledger.jsonl`. A replayed nonce fails.\r\n\r\nThe owner authorization that this key acts under is recorded verbatim in `config/issuer/OWNER_AUTHORIZATION.json`\r\nand its sha is bound into every grant, so a grant can always be traced to the sentence that permitted it.\r\n\r\n  issue_grant.py init\r\n  issue_grant.py issue --principal P --operation install_occupant --writeset WS.json [--ttl 900]\r\n  issue_grant.py verify --grant G.json\r\n  issue_grant.py public-key\r\n\"\"\"\r\n\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\n\r\ntry:  # every child of this process must be consoleless; see is bound to this Windows logon, not to a typed secret.\"\"\"\r\n    if os.name != \"nt\":\r\n        return data\r\n    try:\r\n        import ctypes\r\n        from ctypes import wintypes\r\n\r\n        class DATA_BLOB(ctypes.Structure):\r\n            _fields_ = [(\"cbData\", wintypes.DWORD), (\"pbData\", ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_char))]\r\n\r\n        def blob(b: bytes) -> DATA_BLOB:\r\n            buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(b, len(b))\r\n            return DATA_BLOB(len(b), ctypes.cast(buf, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_char)))\r\n\r\n        def blob_bytes(bl: DATA_BLOB) -> bytes:\r\n            return ctypes.string_at(bl.pbData, bl.cbData)\r\n\r\n        crypt32 = ctypes.windll.crypt32\r\n        fn = crypt32.CryptProtectData if protect else crypt32.CryptUnprotectData\r\n        src, return data\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _key() -> Ed25519PrivateKey:\r\n    if not KEY_PATH.is_file():\r\n        raise SystemExit(\"no issuer key; run `issue_grant.py init` first\")\r\n    raw =                           format=serialization.PublicFormat.Raw)\r\n    return base64.b64encode(pub).decode()\r\n\r\n\r\ndef key_id() -> str:\r\n    pub = must prove parity, and a refused swap must leave the world as it found it.\r\n\r\nWP-FHS-13's first-fail is `CAPABILITY_PARITY_NOT_PROVEN` -- \"熱替代 Issuer 或以選型當 invoke\". The point of the\r\nclause is that a swap is not a capability change until the new occupant SERVES and the old one STOPS serving.\r\nThese tests hold both directions, plus the rollback, because a swap
